If the CIDR is empty, runtimes\n    // should omit it.\n    string pod_cidr = 1;\n}\n\nmessage RuntimeConfig {\n    NetworkConfig network_config = 1;\n}\n\nmessage UpdateRuntimeConfigRequest {\n    RuntimeConfig runtime_config = 1;\n}\n\nmessage UpdateRuntimeConfigResponse {}\n\n// RuntimeCondition contains condition information for the runtime.\n// There are 2 kinds of runtime conditions:\n// 1. Required conditions: Conditions are required for kubelet to work\n// properly. If any required condition is unmet, the node will be not ready.\n// The required conditions include:\n//   * RuntimeReady: RuntimeReady means the runtime is up and ready to accept\n//   basic containers e.g. container only needs host network.\n//   * NetworkReady: NetworkReady means the runtime network is up and ready to\n//   accept containers which require container network.\n// 2. Optional conditions: Conditions are informative to the user, but kubelet\n// will not rely on. Since condition type is an arbitrary string, all conditions\n// not required are optional. These conditions will be exposed to users to help\n// them understand the status of the system.\nmessage RuntimeCondition {\n    // Type of runtime condition.\n    string type = 1;\n    // Status of the condition, one of true/false. Default: false.\n    bool status = 2;\n    // Brief CamelCase string containing reason for the condition's last transition.\n    string reason = 3;\n    // Human-readable message indicating details about last transition.\n    string message = 4;\n}\n\n// RuntimeStatus is information about the current status of the runtime.\nmessage RuntimeStatus {\n    // List of current observed runtime conditions.\n    repeated RuntimeCondition conditions = 1;\n}\n\nmessage StatusRequest {\n    // Verbose indicates whether to return extra information about the runtime.\n    bool verbose = 1;\n}\n\n// RuntimeHandlerFeatures is a set of features implemented by the runtime handler.\nmessage RuntimeHandlerFeatures {\n    // recursive_read_only_mounts is set to true if the runtime handler supports\n    // recursive read-only mounts.\n    // For runc-compatible runtimes, availability of this feature can be detected by checking whether\n    // the Linux kernel version is >= 5.12, and,  `runc features | jq .mountOptions` contains \"rro\".\n    bool recursive_read_only_mounts = 1;\n\n    // user_namespaces is set to true if the runtime handler supports user namespaces as implemented\n    // in Kubernetes. This means support for both, user namespaces and idmap mounts.\n    bool user_namespaces = 2;\n}\n\nmessage RuntimeHandler {\n    // Name must be unique in StatusResponse.\n    // An empty string denotes the default handler.\n    string name = 1;\n    // Supported features.\n    RuntimeHandlerFeatures features = 2;\n}\n\n// RuntimeFeatures describes the set of features implemented by the CRI implementation.\n// The features contained in the RuntimeFeatures should depend only on the cri implementation\n// independent of runtime handlers.\nmessage RuntimeFeatures {\n    // supplemental_groups_policy is set to true if the runtime supports SupplementalGroupsPolicy and ContainerUser.\n    bool supplemental_groups_policy = 1;\n}\n\nmessage StatusResponse {\n    // Status of the Runtime.\n    RuntimeStatus status = 1;\n    // Info is extra information of the Runtime. The key could be arbitrary string, and\n    // value should be in json format. The information could include anything useful for\n    // debug, e.g. plugins used by the container runtime.\n    // It should only be returned non-empty when Verbose is true.\n    map<string, string> info = 2;\n    // Runtime handlers.\n    repeated RuntimeHandler runtime_handlers = 3;\n    // features describes the set of features implemented by the CRI implementation.\n    // This field is supposed to propagate to NodeFeatures in Kubernetes API.\n    RuntimeFeatures features = 4;\n}\n\nmessage ImageFsInfoRequest {}\n\n// UInt64Value is the wrapper of uint64.\nmessage UInt64Value {\n    // The value.\n    uint64 value = 1;\n}\n\n// FilesystemIdentifier uniquely identify the filesystem.\nmessage FilesystemIdentifier{\n    // Mountpoint of a filesystem.\n    string mountpoint = 1;\n}\n\n// FilesystemUsage provides the filesystem usage information.\nmessage FilesystemUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // The unique identifier of the filesystem.\n    FilesystemIdentifier fs_id = 2;\n    // UsedBytes represents the bytes used for images on the filesystem.\n    // This may differ from the total bytes used on the filesystem and may not\n    // equal CapacityBytes - AvailableBytes.\n    UInt64Value used_bytes = 3;\n    // InodesUsed represents the inodes used by the images.\n    // This may not equal InodesCapacity - InodesAvailable because the underlying\n    // filesystem may also be used for purposes other than storing images.\n    UInt64Value inodes_used = 4;\n}\n\n// WindowsFilesystemUsage provides the filesystem usage information specific to Windows.\nmessage WindowsFilesystemUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // The unique identifier of the filesystem.\n    FilesystemIdentifier fs_id = 2;\n    // UsedBytes represents the bytes used for images on the filesystem.\n    // This may differ from the total bytes used on the filesystem and may not\n    // equal CapacityBytes - AvailableBytes.\n    UInt64Value used_bytes = 3;\n}\n\nmessage ImageFsInfoResponse {\n    // Information of image filesystem(s).\n    repeated FilesystemUsage image_filesystems = 1;\n    // Information of container filesystem(s).\n    // This is an optional field, may be used for example if container and image\n    // storage are separated.\n    // Default will be to return this as empty.\n    repeated FilesystemUsage container_filesystems = 2;\n}\n\nmessage ContainerStatsRequest{\n    // ID of the container for which to retrieve stats.\n    string container_id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage ContainerStatsResponse {\n    // Stats of the container.\n    ContainerStats stats = 1;\n}\n\nmessage ListContainerStatsRequest{\n    // Filter for the list request.\n    ContainerStatsFilter filter = 1;\n}\n\n// ContainerStatsFilter is used to filter containers.\n// All those fields are combined with 'AND'\nmessage ContainerStatsFilter {\n    // ID of the container.\n    string id = 1;\n    // ID of the PodSandbox.\n    string pod_sandbox_id = 2;\n    // LabelSelector to select matches.\n    // Only api.MatchLabels is supported for now and the requirements\n    // are ANDed. ContainerMetadata metadata = 2;\n    // Key-value pairs that may be used to scope and select individual resources.\n    map<string,string> labels = 3;\n    // Unstructured key-value map holding arbitrary metadata.\n    // Annotations MUST NOT be altered by the runtime; the value of this field\n    // MUST be identical to that of the corresponding ContainerConfig used to\n    // instantiate the Container this status represents.\n    map<string,string> annotations = 4;\n}\n\n// ContainerStats provides the resource usage statistics for a container.\nmessage ContainerStats {\n    // Information of the container.\n    ContainerAttributes attributes = 1;\n    // CPU usage gathered from the container.\n    CpuUsage cpu = 2;\n    // Memory usage gathered from the container.\n    MemoryUsage memory = 3;\n    // Usage of the writable layer.\n    FilesystemUsage writable_layer = 4;\n    // Swap usage gathered from the container.\n    SwapUsage swap = 5;\n    // IO usage gathered from the container.\n    IoUsage io = 6;\n}\n\n// WindowsContainerStats provides the resource usage statistics for a container specific for Windows\nmessage WindowsContainerStats {\n    // Information of the container.\n    ContainerAttributes attributes = 1;\n    // CPU usage gathered from the container.\n    WindowsCpuUsage cpu = 2;\n    // Memory usage gathered from the container.\n    WindowsMemoryUsage memory = 3;\n    // Usage of the writable layer.\n    WindowsFilesystemUsage writable_layer = 4;\n}\n\n// PSI statistics for an individual resource.\nmessage PsiStats {\n\t// PSI data for all tasks in the cgroup.\n\tPsiData Full = 1;\n\t// PSI data for some tasks in the cgroup.\n\tPsiData Some = 2;\n}\n\n// PSI data for an individual resource.\nmessage PsiData {\n\t// Total time duration for tasks in the cgroup have waited due to congestion.\n\t// Unit: nanoseconds.\n\tuint64 Total = 1;\n\t// The average (in %) tasks have waited due to congestion over a 10 second window.\n\tdouble Avg10 = 2;\n\t// The average (in %) tasks have waited due to congestion over a 60 second window.\n\tdouble Avg60 = 3;\n\t// The average (in %) tasks have waited due to congestion over a 300 second window.\n\tdouble Avg300 = 4;\n}\n\n// CpuUsage provides the CPU usage information.\nmessage CpuUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // Cumulative CPU usage (sum across all cores) since object creation.\n    UInt64Value usage_core_nano_seconds = 2;\n    // Total CPU usage (sum of all cores) averaged over the sample window.\n    // The \"core\" unit can be interpreted as CPU core-nanoseconds per second.\n    UInt64Value usage_nano_cores = 3;\n    // CPU PSI statistics.\n    PsiStats psi = 4;\n}\n\n// WindowsCpuUsage provides the CPU usage information specific to Windows\nmessage WindowsCpuUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // Cumulative CPU usage (sum across all cores) since object creation.\n    UInt64Value usage_core_nano_seconds = 2;\n    // Total CPU usage (sum of all cores) averaged over the sample window.\n    // The \"core\" unit can be interpreted as CPU core-nanoseconds per second.\n    UInt64Value usage_nano_cores = 3;\n}\n\n// MemoryUsage provides the memory usage information.\nmessage MemoryUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // The amount of working set memory in bytes.\n    UInt64Value working_set_bytes = 2;\n    // Available memory for use. This is defined as the memory limit - workingSetBytes.\n    UInt64Value available_bytes = 3;\n    // Total memory in use. This includes all memory regardless of when it was accessed.\n    UInt64Value usage_bytes = 4;\n    // The amount of anonymous and swap cache memory (includes transparent hugepages).\n    UInt64Value rss_bytes = 5;\n    // Cumulative number of minor page faults.\n    UInt64Value page_faults = 6;\n    // Cumulative number of major page faults.\n    UInt64Value major_page_faults = 7;\n    // Memory PSI statistics.\n    PsiStats psi = 8;\n}\n\nmessage IoUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // Available swap for use. This is defined as the swap limit - swapUsageBytes.\n    UInt64Value swap_available_bytes = 2;\n    // Total memory in use. This includes all memory regardless of when it was accessed.\n    UInt64Value swap_usage_bytes = 3;\n}\n\n// WindowsMemoryUsage provides the memory usage information specific to Windows\nmessage WindowsMemoryUsage {\n    // Timestamp in nanoseconds at which the information were collected. Must be > 0.\n    int64 timestamp = 1;\n    // The amount of working set memory in bytes.\n    UInt64Value working_set_bytes = 2;\n    // Available memory for use. This is defined as the memory limit - commit_memory_bytes.\n    UInt64Value available_bytes = 3;\n    // Cumulative number of page faults.\n    UInt64Value page_faults = 4;\n    // Total commit memory in use. Commit memory is total of physical and virtual memory in use.\n    UInt64Value commit_memory_bytes = 5;\n}\n\nmessage ReopenContainerLogRequest {\n    // ID of the container for which to reopen the log.\n    string container_id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage ReopenContainerLogResponse{\n}\n\nmessage CheckpointContainerRequest {\n    // ID of the container to be checkpointed.\n    string container_id = 1;\n    // Location of the checkpoint archive used for export\n    string location = 2;\n    // Timeout in seconds for the checkpoint to complete.\n    // Timeout of zero means to use the CRI default.\n    // Timeout > 0 means to use the user specified timeout.\n    int64 timeout = 3;\n}\n\nmessage CheckpointContainerResponse {}\n\nmessage GetEventsRequest {}\n\nmessage ContainerEventResponse {\n    // Key RPC Methods and Their Purpose\n\n### Pod Sandbox Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `RunPodSandbox` | Creates and starts a pod-level sandbox (network namespace, cgroups) |\n| `StopPodSandbox` | Stops sandbox processes and reclaims network resources |\n| `RemovePodSandbox` | Removes the sandbox completely |\n| `PodSandboxStatus` | Returns status including IP addresses, state |\n| `ListPodSandbox` | Lists all sandboxes with optional filtering |\n| `PodSandboxStats` | Returns resource usage metrics for a sandbox |\n| `UpdatePodSandboxResources` | Updates cgroup resource constraints |\n| `PortForward` | Sets up port forwarding to a sandbox |\n\n### Container Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `CreateContainer` | Creates a container in a specified pod sandbox |\n| `StartContainer` | Starts a created container |\n| `StopContainer` | Stops a running container with grace period |\n| `RemoveContainer` | Removes a container |\n| `ListContainers` | Lists containers with filtering |\n| `ContainerStatus` | Returns container state, exit code, timestamps |\n| `UpdateContainerResources` | Updates container cgroup limits |\n| `ReopenContainerLog` | Reopens stdout/stderr log files |\n\n### Exec/Streaming Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `ExecSync` | Runs command synchronously, returns output |\n| `Exec` | Prepares streaming endpoint for command execution |\n| `Attach` | Attaches to running container's stdin/stdout/stderr |\n\n### Image Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `ListImages` | Lists all images |\n| `ImageStatus` | Returns image details (size, digests) |\n| `PullImage` | Pulls image with authentication |\n| `RemoveImage` | Removes an image |\n| `ImageFsInfo` | Returns filesystem usage for images |\n\n## 3. Containerd's CRI Implementation\n\n### Plugin Registration\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/plugins/cri/cri.go`\n\nContainerd registers its CRI implementation as a gRPC plugin:\n\n```go\nfunc init() {\n    registry.Register(&plugin.Registration{\n        Type: plugins.GRPCPlugin,\n        ID:   \"cri\",\n        Requires: []plugin.Type{\n            plugins.CRIServicePlugin,\n            plugins.PodSandboxPlugin,\n            plugins.SandboxControllerPlugin,\n            plugins.NRIApiPlugin,\n            plugins.EventPlugin,\n            plugins.ServicePlugin,\n            plugins.LeasePlugin,\n            plugins.SandboxStorePlugin,\n            plugins.TransferPlugin,\n            plugins.WarningPlugin,\n        },\n        InitFn: initCRIService,\n    })\n}\n```\n\nThe plugin imports the CRI proto types directly from Kubernetes:\n```go\nimport runtime \"k8s.io/cri-api/pkg/apis/runtime/v1\"\n```\n\n### Core Service Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/service.go`\n\nThe `criService` struct implements both `RuntimeServiceServer` and `ImageServiceServer`:\n\n```go\ntype criService struct {\n    runtime.UnimplementedRuntimeServiceServer\n    runtime.UnimplementedImageServiceServer\n\n    config             criconfig.Config\n    sandboxStore       *sandboxstore.Store\n    containerStore     *containerstore.Store\n    sandboxNameIndex   *registrar.Registrar\n    containerNameIndex *registrar.Registrar\n    netPlugin          map[string]cni.CNI\n    client             *containerd.Client\n    streamServer       streaming.Server\n    eventMonitor       *events.EventMonitor\n    // ... additional fields\n}\n```\n\n### CRI Method Implementations\n\nEach CRI RPC method is implemented in a dedicated file:\n\n| CRI Method | Implementation File | Line |\n|------------|---------------------|------|\n| `RunPodSandbox` | `sandbox_run.go` | 53 |\n| `StopPodSandbox` | `sandbox_stop.go` | - |\n| `RemovePodSandbox` | `sandbox_remove.go` | - |\n| `PodSandboxStatus` | `sandbox_status.go` | - |\n| `ListPodSandbox` | `sandbox_list.go` | - |\n| `CreateContainer` | `container_create.go` | 60 |\n| `StartContainer` | `container_start.go` | 45 |\n| `StopContainer` | `container_stop.go` | 39 |\n| `RemoveContainer` | `container_remove.go` | 34 |\n| `ListContainers` | `container_list.go` | - |\n| `ContainerStatus` | `container_status.go` | - |\n| `ExecSync` | `container_execsync.go` | - |\n| `Exec` | `container_exec.go` | - |\n| `Attach` | `container_attach.go` | - |\n\n### Example: RunPodSandbox Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/sandbox_run.go:53`\n\n```go\nfunc (c *criService) RunPodSandbox(ctx context.Context, r *runtime.RunPodSandboxRequest) (_ *runtime.RunPodSandboxResponse, retErr error) {\n    config := r.GetConfig()\n\n    // 1. Generate unique ID and reserve sandbox name\n    id := util.GenerateID()\n    name := makeSandboxName(metadata)\n    c.sandboxNameIndex.Reserve(name, id)\n\n    // 2. Create lease for resource management\n    ls, _ := leaseSvc.Create(ctx, leases.WithID(id))\n\n    // 3. Get OCI runtime configuration\n    ociRuntime, _ := c.config.GetSandboxRuntime(config, r.GetRuntimeHandler())\n\n    // 4. Create sandbox metadata\n    sandbox := sandboxstore.NewSandbox(...)\n\n    // 5. Setup network namespace (if not host network)\n    if !hostNetwork(config) {\n        sandbox.NetNS, _ = netns.NewNetNS(netnsMountDir)\n        c.setupPodNetwork(ctx, &sandbox)\n    }\n\n    // 6. Create and start sandbox via sandbox controller\n    c.sandboxService.CreateSandbox(ctx, sandboxInfo, ...)\n    ctrl, _ := c.sandboxService.StartSandbox(ctx, sandbox.Sandboxer, id)\n\n    // 7. Return sandbox ID\n    return &runtime.RunPodSandboxResponse{PodSandboxId: id}, nil\n}\n```\n\n### Example: CreateContainer Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/container_create.go:60`\n\n```go\nfunc (c *criService) CreateContainer(ctx context.Context, r *runtime.CreateContainerRequest) (_ *runtime.CreateContainerResponse, retErr error) {\n    config := r.GetConfig()\n    sandboxConfig := r.GetSandboxConfig()\n    sandbox, _ := c.sandboxStore.Get(r.GetPodSandboxId())\n\n    // 1. Generate container ID and reserve name\n    id := util.GenerateID()\n    name := makeContainerName(metadata, sandboxConfig.GetMetadata())\n\n    // 2. Build OCI runtime spec (platform-specific)\n    spec, _ := c.buildContainerSpec(...)  // calls buildLinuxSpec, buildWindowsSpec, etc.\n\n    // 3. Create container in containerd\n    container, _ := c.client.NewContainer(ctx, id, containerOpts...)\n\n    // 4. Store container metadata\n    c.containerStore.Add(containerstore.Container{...})\n\n    return &runtime.CreateContainerResponse{ContainerId: id}, nil\n}\n```\n\n## 4. Kubernetes Vendored containerd Types\n\n### Vendored Packages\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/`\n\nKubernetes vendors the following containerd packages (from `go.mod`):\n\n```\ngithub.com/containerd/containerd/api v1.10.0\ngithub.com/containerd/errdefs v1.0.0\ngithub.com/containerd/errdefs/pkg v0.3.0\ngithub.com/containerd/log v0.1.0\ngithub.com/containerd/ttrpc v1.2.7\ngithub.com/containerd/typeurl/v2 v2.2.3\n```\n\n### Vendored Directory Structure\n\n```\nkubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/\n├── containerd/\n│   └── api/\n│       ├── services/\n│       │   ├── containers/v1/   # Container management service\n│       │   ├── tasks/v1/        # Task execution service\n│       │   └── version/v1/      # Version info service\n│       └── types/\n│           ├── task/            # Task types (Status, Process)\n│           ├── descriptor.proto # OCI descriptor types\n│           ├── mount.proto      # Mount configuration\n│           ├── platform.proto   # Platform specifications\n│           ├── metrics.proto    # Logging utilities\n├── ttrpc/                       # UNIX socket RPC protocol\n└── typeurl/v2/                  # Type URL marshaling\n```\n\n### Key Vendored Types\n\n**Task Status** (`api/types/task/task.proto`):\n```protobuf\nenum Status {\n    UNKNOWN = 0;\n    CREATED = 1;\n    RUNNING = 2;\n    STOPPED = 3;\n    PAUSED = 4;\n    PAUSING = 5;\n}\n```\n\n**Container** (`api/services/containers/v1/containers.proto`):\n```protobuf\nmessage Container {\n    string id\n    map<string, string> labels\n    string image\n    Container.Runtime runtime\n    google.protobuf.Any spec\n    string snapshotter\n    string snapshot_key\n    string sandbox\n}\n```\n\n**Mount** (`api/types/mount.proto`):\n```protobuf\nmessage Mount {\n    string type\n    string source\n    string target\n    repeated string options\n}\n```\n\n### ttrpc vs gRPC\n\nKubernetes vendors both communication protocols:\n- **gRPC**: Standard protocol for network communication\n- **ttrpc**: Lightweight typed UNIX socket RPC for local communication\n\ncontainerd supports both, with ttrpc providing lower overhead for local kubelet-to-containerd communication.\n\n## 5. Interface Contract Flow\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                           KUBERNETES                                     │\n│ Key Implementation Details\n\n### Idempotent Operations\nStop and Remove operations are idempotent - they don't return errors if resources are already in the desired state. This is required by the CRI specification.\n\n### Name Uniqueness\nBoth sandbox and container names are tracked via `registrar.Registrar` to ensure uniqueness across the namespace.\n\n### Platform Support\ncontainerd provides platform-specific implementations:\n- `container_create_linux.go`\n- `container_create_windows.go`\n- `container_create_other.go`\n\n### Resource Management\nLeases are used to track resource lifecycle, ensuring cleanup even on failures.\n\n### Instrumentation\nThe `instrument` package wraps all CRI calls for metrics and tracing:\n```go\nfunc (c *Old_criGRPCServer) register(s *grpc.Server) error {\n    instrumented := instrument.NewService(c)\n    runtime.RegisterRuntimeServiceServer(s, instrumented)\n    runtime.RegisterImageServiceServer(s, instrumented)\n    return nil\n}\n```\n\n## Summary\n\nThe CRI interface provides a clean contract between Kubernetes and container runtimes:\n\n1. **Kubernetes** defines the CRI proto at `staging/src/k8s.io/cri-api/pkg/apis/runtime/v1/api.proto`\n2. **containerd** implements the CRI as a plugin at `plugins/cri/cri.go`\n3. Key RPC Methods and Their Purpose\n\n### Pod Sandbox Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `RunPodSandbox` | Creates and starts a pod-level sandbox (network namespace, cgroups) |\n| `StopPodSandbox` | Stops sandbox processes and reclaims network resources |\n| `RemovePodSandbox` | Removes the sandbox completely |\n| `PodSandboxStatus` | Returns status including IP addresses, state |\n| `ListPodSandbox` | Lists all sandboxes with optional filtering |\n| `PodSandboxStats` | Returns resource usage metrics for a sandbox |\n| `UpdatePodSandboxResources` | Updates cgroup resource constraints |\n| `PortForward` | Sets up port forwarding to a sandbox |\n\n### Container Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `CreateContainer` | Creates a container in a specified pod sandbox |\n| `StartContainer` | Starts a created container |\n| `StopContainer` | Stops a running container with grace period |\n| `RemoveContainer` | Removes a container |\n| `ListContainers` | Lists containers with filtering |\n| `ContainerStatus` | Returns container state, exit code, timestamps |\n| `UpdateContainerResources` | Updates container cgroup limits |\n| `ReopenContainerLog` | Reopens stdout/stderr log files |\n\n### Exec/Streaming Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `ExecSync` | Runs command synchronously, returns output |\n| `Exec` | Prepares streaming endpoint for command execution |\n| `Attach` | Attaches to running container's stdin/stdout/stderr |\n\n### Image Operations\n\n| RPC Method | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `ListImages` | Lists all images |\n| `ImageStatus` | Returns image details (size, digests) |\n| `PullImage` | Pulls image with authentication |\n| `RemoveImage` | Removes an image |\n| `ImageFsInfo` | Returns filesystem usage for images |\n\n## 3. Containerd's CRI Implementation\n\n### Plugin Registration\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/plugins/cri/cri.go`\n\nContainerd registers its CRI implementation as a gRPC plugin:\n\n```go\nfunc init() {\n    registry.Register(&plugin.Registration{\n        Type: plugins.GRPCPlugin,\n        ID:   \"cri\",\n        Requires: []plugin.Type{\n            plugins.CRIServicePlugin,\n            plugins.PodSandboxPlugin,\n            plugins.SandboxControllerPlugin,\n            plugins.NRIApiPlugin,\n            plugins.EventPlugin,\n            plugins.ServicePlugin,\n            plugins.LeasePlugin,\n            plugins.SandboxStorePlugin,\n            plugins.TransferPlugin,\n            plugins.WarningPlugin,\n        },\n        InitFn: initCRIService,\n    })\n}\n```\n\nThe plugin imports the CRI proto types directly from Kubernetes:\n```go\nimport runtime \"k8s.io/cri-api/pkg/apis/runtime/v1\"\n```\n\n### Core Service Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/service.go`\n\nThe `criService` struct implements both `RuntimeServiceServer` and `ImageServiceServer`:\n\n```go\ntype criService struct {\n    runtime.UnimplementedRuntimeServiceServer\n    runtime.UnimplementedImageServiceServer\n\n    config             criconfig.Config\n    sandboxStore       *sandboxstore.Store\n    containerStore     *containerstore.Store\n    sandboxNameIndex   *registrar.Registrar\n    containerNameIndex *registrar.Registrar\n    netPlugin          map[string]cni.CNI\n    client             *containerd.Client\n    streamServer       streaming.Server\n    eventMonitor       *events.EventMonitor\n    // ... additional fields\n}\n```\n\n### CRI Method Implementations\n\nEach CRI RPC method is implemented in a dedicated file:\n\n| CRI Method | Implementation File | Line |\n|------------|---------------------|------|\n| `RunPodSandbox` | `sandbox_run.go` | 53 |\n| `StopPodSandbox` | `sandbox_stop.go` | - |\n| `RemovePodSandbox` | `sandbox_remove.go` | - |\n| `PodSandboxStatus` | `sandbox_status.go` | - |\n| `ListPodSandbox` | `sandbox_list.go` | - |\n| `CreateContainer` | `container_create.go` | 60 |\n| `StartContainer` | `container_start.go` | 45 |\n| `StopContainer` | `container_stop.go` | 39 |\n| `RemoveContainer` | `container_remove.go` | 34 |\n| `ListContainers` | `container_list.go` | - |\n| `ContainerStatus` | `container_status.go` | - |\n| `ExecSync` | `container_execsync.go` | - |\n| `Exec` | `container_exec.go` | - |\n| `Attach` | `container_attach.go` | - |\n\n### Example: RunPodSandbox Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/sandbox_run.go:53`\n\n```go\nfunc (c *criService) RunPodSandbox(ctx context.Context, r *runtime.RunPodSandboxRequest) (_ *runtime.RunPodSandboxResponse, retErr error) {\n    config := r.GetConfig()\n\n    // 1. Generate unique ID and reserve sandbox name\n    id := util.GenerateID()\n    name := makeSandboxName(metadata)\n    c.sandboxNameIndex.Reserve(name, id)\n\n    // 2. Create lease for resource management\n    ls, _ := leaseSvc.Create(ctx, leases.WithID(id))\n\n    // 3. Get OCI runtime configuration\n    ociRuntime, _ := c.config.GetSandboxRuntime(config, r.GetRuntimeHandler())\n\n    // 4. Create sandbox metadata\n    sandbox := sandboxstore.NewSandbox(...)\n\n    // 5. Setup network namespace (if not host network)\n    if !hostNetwork(config) {\n        sandbox.NetNS, _ = netns.NewNetNS(netnsMountDir)\n        c.setupPodNetwork(ctx, &sandbox)\n    }\n\n    // 6. Create and start sandbox via sandbox controller\n    c.sandboxService.CreateSandbox(ctx, sandboxInfo, ...)\n    ctrl, _ := c.sandboxService.StartSandbox(ctx, sandbox.Sandboxer, id)\n\n    // 7. Return sandbox ID\n    return &runtime.RunPodSandboxResponse{PodSandboxId: id}, nil\n}\n```\n\n### Example: CreateContainer Implementation\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/containerd/internal/cri/server/container_create.go:60`\n\n```go\nfunc (c *criService) CreateContainer(ctx context.Context, r *runtime.CreateContainerRequest) (_ *runtime.CreateContainerResponse, retErr error) {\n    config := r.GetConfig()\n    sandboxConfig := r.GetSandboxConfig()\n    sandbox, _ := c.sandboxStore.Get(r.GetPodSandboxId())\n\n    // 1. Generate container ID and reserve name\n    id := util.GenerateID()\n    name := makeContainerName(metadata, sandboxConfig.GetMetadata())\n\n    // 2. Build OCI runtime spec (platform-specific)\n    spec, _ := c.buildContainerSpec(...)  // calls buildLinuxSpec, buildWindowsSpec, etc.\n\n    // 3. Create container in containerd\n    container, _ := c.client.NewContainer(ctx, id, containerOpts...)\n\n    // 4. Store container metadata\n    c.containerStore.Add(containerstore.Container{...})\n\n    return &runtime.CreateContainerResponse{ContainerId: id}, nil\n}\n```\n\n## 4. Kubernetes Vendored containerd Types\n\n### Vendored Packages\n\n**Location:** `/ccb_crossrepo/src/kubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/`\n\nKubernetes vendors the following containerd packages (from `go.mod`):\n\n```\ngithub.com/containerd/containerd/api v1.10.0\ngithub.com/containerd/errdefs v1.0.0\ngithub.com/containerd/errdefs/pkg v0.3.0\ngithub.com/containerd/log v0.1.0\ngithub.com/containerd/ttrpc v1.2.7\ngithub.com/containerd/typeurl/v2 v2.2.3\n```\n\n### Vendored Directory Structure\n\n```\nkubernetes/vendor/github.com/containerd/\n├── containerd/\n│   └── api/\n│       ├── services/\n│       │   ├── containers/v1/   #
